# Ben Horowitz (p0003)

## Review approach

Compared BEN and BENJAMIN groups using a16z/AH Capital and founder/partner roles, independently of California/Nevada geography. BENJAMIN A/ABRAHAM is compatible with this cluster. BENJAMIN E at Venable and BENJAMIN at Workday are rejected collision cases: Venable independently identifies the Washington lawyer, while Workday engineering conflicts with the investor career. Foundation, Horowitz Group, missing-role and swapped-field records require more evidence. Source-company legal-name evidence is cited separately from the individual's biography.

Examined 144 candidate evidence groups containing 530 source records. Accepted 460 record links. Counts are not distinct gifts or dollar totals.

## Identity and collision sources

- [Andreessen Horowitz: About](https://a16z.com/about/) — Identifies Ben Horowitz and Marc Andreessen as the founders of a16z in 2009. Observed 2026-09-20.
- [a16z: primary business legal name](https://a16z.com/were-leaving-delaware-and-we-think-you-should-consider-leaving-too/) — a16z identifies AH Capital Management as its primary business in its 2025 incorporation announcement. Observed 2026-09-20.
- [Ben Horowitz: a16z career biography](https://a16z.com/author/ben-horowitz/) — a16z identifies Ben as its cofounder/general partner and describes his Opsware, HP, AOL and Netscape career. Observed 2026-09-20.
- [Collision: Benjamin E. Horowitz, Venable attorney](https://www.venable.com/professionals/h/benjamin-e-horowitz) — Venable identifies Benjamin E. Horowitz as a Washington litigation partner with a 2012 JD; this is an alternative professional profile to the a16z founder. Observed 2026-09-20.

## Scope and unresolved work

Only the exact records and hashes listed below are covered. Known name variants and initials were retrieved across both installed cycles without a giving threshold. Unlisted surname typos, changed surnames and unitemized giving are not covered. Acceptance never changes reported employer or gift accounting. Initial decisions were prepared by Codex using explicitly documented person-specific criteria and then expanded to evidence groups; they are not human sign-offs.
